Software Quality Assurance (SQA) Final Report and SRS Summary
=============================================================

Project: Quality Assured Local Guide Platform
Author: Faisal Mahmud
Student ID: 2517729650
Course: CSE 534, Section 1
Institution: North South University
Application Stack: MERN (MongoDB, Express, React, Node.js)

1. Software Requirements Specification (SRS) Summary
====================================================

System Overview
The Quality Assured Local Guide Platform is a MERN-stack tourism marketplace application where tourists discover local experiences, guides publish and manage tours, and administrators govern users and platform activity. The backend exposes REST APIs for authentication, tours, bookings, reviews, messages, and administration. The frontend provides public browsing, protected dashboards, tour details, search, booking, registration, login, and profile capabilities.

System Architecture
1. Presentation Layer: React frontend with BrowserRouter, protected routes, dashboards, public pages, listing pages, tour details, registration, login, and profile pages.
2. Application Layer: Node.js and Express services implementing business rules, search/filtering, booking validation, authorization, and admin operations.
3. Data Layer: MongoDB with Mongoose models for User, Tour, Booking, and Review, including relational references and lifecycle cleanup behavior enforced in application logic.

Core Functional Requirements by User Role
Tourist:
- Register and log in as a Tourist.
- Browse tours and filter by location, language, category, date, and price-oriented criteria.
- View tour details.
- Submit booking requests for available dates.
- View personal bookings from dashboard and booking endpoints.
- Submit reviews and participate in messaging flows.

Guide:
- Register and log in as a Guide.
- Access guide dashboard.
- Create tours with title, description, location, category, language, price, availability dates, and image support.
- View and manage bookings for owned tours.
- Approve booking requests.
- Maintain tour inventory and guide-facing dashboard state.

Admin:
- Log in as Admin.
- Access admin-only dashboard and routes.
- View all users without returning password fields.
- Delete users.
- Trigger cleanup of dependent tours, bookings, and reviews where applicable.
- Retrieve aggregate platform statistics for users, tours, and bookings.

Core Modules
1. Authentication: Registration, login, JWT issuance, protected routes, and role persistence.
2. Tour Management: Tour search, listing, detail retrieval, creation, update, delete, pagination, and filtering.
3. Booking Workflow: Tourist booking requests, guide approval, my-bookings retrieval, date validation, and conflict prevention.
4. Real-time Messaging/Reviews: Messaging endpoints are mounted in the backend; reviews are linked to tours and tourists and are included in data integrity checks.

Non-Functional Requirements
Security: JWT-based authentication, RBAC for Tourist/Guide/Admin, malformed-token rejection, unauthorized route denial, and resilience against hostile query-string content.
Performance: Query pagination, filtered search, high-volume automated case coverage, and responsive dashboard/listing interactions.
Reliability: In-memory isolated test databases for backend testing, cascade cleanup validation, regression protection, and documented handling of intermittent E2E environment instability.

2. Test Strategy & Methodology
==============================

Backend Testing Strategy
The backend test program uses Jest with mongodb-memory-server to execute isolated automated tests without polluting the production database. This strategy covers unit tests, bulk system-style boundary tests, integration tests across models and routes, and regression protection for previously fixed defects. Supertest is used to exercise actual Express routes with realistic HTTP requests and JWT-protected access paths.

Frontend / E2E Strategy
The frontend quality program uses Playwright and a Page Object Model design pattern for critical-path interactions such as registration, login, guide dashboard operations, tour listing search, tour detail booking, and protected navigation. In addition to direct scenario flows, the suite contains high-volume generated module packs for regression, smoke, and security-oriented browser checks.

Methodological Characteristics
- Positive, negative, boundary, combinational, regression, and role-based access tests are present.
- Backend assertions verify both HTTP output and database state where appropriate.
- Frontend assertions verify URL transitions, DOM state, visibility, route protection, validation behavior, and search/navigation interactions.

3. Testing Architecture & Folder Structure
==========================================

Backend Test Tree: /tests
- /tests/unit: controller and middleware unit coverage.
- /tests/integration: middleware + RBAC, booking flow, data integrity, and tour search/filter integration behavior.
- /tests/system: route-level system verification for admin, auth, booking, and tour modules.
- /tests/regression: previously fixed issues protected from reintroduction.
- /tests/setup: shared in-memory database bootstrap and cleanup support.

Frontend Test Tree: /testing/tests/e2e
- bookingWorkflow.spec.js: direct critical-path Playwright journey coverage.
- /modules/*.spec.js: high-volume generated packs for authentication, booking, dashboard, navigation, registration, security, tour detail, and tour listing behaviors.
- Playwright assertions are organized around browser-visible behavior and protected routing expectations.

4. EXHAUSTIVE Test Case Execution & Results
===========================================

Execution Note
For the four critical-path Playwright cases, repository documentation includes explicit recorded outcomes. For the generated module packs and committed Jest suites, the table below records the exact automated assertion behavior present in source, with pass/fail reported against the committed automation baseline unless repository evidence explicitly records a failure.

4.1 FRONTEND E2E - testing/tests/e2e/bookingWorkflow.spec.js
------------------------------------------------------------

Source File: testing/tests/e2e/bookingWorkflow.spec.js
Describe Block(s):
- Local Guide Platform critical path

| Test ID | Module/Category | Test Description / Steps | Expected Outcome | Actual Output/Result | Status (Pass/Fail) | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E2E-BWF-001 | Booking Workflow / Critical Path | Register Tourist with unique email, verify redirect to /dashboard/tourist, logout, log in again, verify dashboard navigation is visible. | Tourist account is created, authenticated, redirected to tourist dashboard, and can log in again successfully. | Repository execution report records intermittent failure: register form became non-interactable and timed out on getByLabel(Name). | Fail | High |
| E2E-BWF-002 | Tour Management / Critical Path | Register Guide, open guide dashboard, create tour, navigate to public tours, search Coxs Bazar, verify created tour title appears. | Guide can create a tour and the tour becomes visible in public listing search results. | Repository execution report records latest successful completion of tour creation and listing visibility flow. | Pass | High |
| E2E-BWF-003 | Booking Workflow / Approval Flow | Register Guide and create tour, register Tourist, search and open tour, book available date, log in as Guide and approve booking, log back in as Tourist and verify approved status. | End-to-end tourist booking and guide approval flow completes and approved status is visible on tourist side. | Repository execution report records intermittent failure: post-registration redirect remained on /register instead of /dashboard/guide in unstable run. | Fail | Critical |
| E2E-BWF-004 | Validation / Negative Flow | Create tour via guide account, register Tourist, attempt invalid login with wrong password, open tour, and verify past date is disabled in booking UI. | Invalid login displays an alert and the UI prevents selection of past booking dates. | Repository execution report records mixed behavior: invalid-login alert was intermittently not visible, while past-date disabled-date validation was successfully updated and asserted. | Fail | High |

4.10 BACKEND UNIT - tests/unit/adminController.unit.test.js
-----------------------------------------------------------

Source File: tests/unit/adminController.unit.test.js
Describe Block(s):
- Admin Controller Unit Tests

| Test ID | Module/Category | Test Description / Steps | Expected Outcome | Actual Output/Result | Status (Pass/Fail) | Severity |

5. Defect Log & Resolution Report
=================================

| Test ID | Module/Category | Test Description / Steps | Expected Outcome | Actual Output/Result | Status (Pass/Fail) | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DEF-001 | Frontend Navigation / Breadcrumb Rendering | Breadcrumb display introduced in header navigation path. | Supplementary navigation should render correctly without clutter or regression. | Commit 933d16e removed breadcrumb display from header after it became an issue in the active UI flow. | Pass | Medium |
| DEF-002 | Guide Dashboard / Auth Provider and Image Upload Rendering | Guide dashboard was failing around auth-provider state and upload rendering behavior. | Guide dashboard should preserve authenticated state and render image upload correctly. | Commit 8dad2e0 fixed guide dashboard auth provider and image upload rendering. | Pass | Critical |
| DEF-003 | Guide Tour Image Handling | Tour creation image handling and upload behavior were inconsistent. | Guide can upload and retain tour image during creation flow. | Commit 29d806d fixed guide tour image handling and allowed image upload during tour creation. | Pass | High |
| DEF-004 | Booking/Auth Reload and Dashboard State | Reload behavior was breaking booking/auth state and guide dashboard continuity. | Reload should preserve auth state and booking continuity. | Commit b3b3811 fixed booking and auth reload behavior, preserved guide dashboard state, and improved homepage search and tour detail booking. | Pass | Critical |
| DEF-005 | Tour Details Routing Import Defect | TourDetails import placement in App.jsx caused route configuration defect. | TourDetails route import should be declared correctly at module top level. | Commits 0e9ff95 and 3c42d45 normalized TourDetails import usage and fixed route import placement. | Pass | High |
| DEF-006 | PostCSS / Google Fonts Import Order | Style import order violated PostCSS compliance. | Google Fonts import should appear before Tailwind processing directives. | Commit 04c0eae moved @import for Google Fonts above Tailwind in style.css. | Pass | Medium |
| DEF-007 | RBAC, Tour Search, Booking, and Cascade Cleanup | Backend issues existed across access control, booking logic, search filtering, and cleanup integrity. | Core backend behaviors should align with requirements and automated tests. | Commit 96addc0 added integration test suite and backend fixes for RBAC, tour search, booking, and cascading deletes. | Pass | Critical |
| DEF-008 | E2E Stability / Negative Validation | Intermittent E2E failures occurred in booking workflow and negative validation checks. | Browser flows should remain stable across identical runs. | SQA execution report documents intermittent failures, stronger waits, selector robustness, and UI-level disabled-date validation updates as mitigation rather than full elimination. | Fail | High |

6. Conclusion & Quality Sign-Off
================================

Overall Assessment
The Quality Assured Local Guide Platform demonstrates strong layered automated coverage across frontend browser flows and backend logic, including unit, integration, system, regression, and bulk combinational testing. Core requirements for Tourist, Guide, and Admin roles are represented in the source and validated through automated assertions. Security controls such as JWT-based authentication, RBAC enforcement, protected-route denial, query safety, and duplicate-booking prevention are explicitly covered.

Deployment Readiness
From a functional and backend-quality perspective, the platform is substantially ready for controlled deployment, especially for API-level stability and core business rules. The principal residual risk is frontend E2E environment instability documented in the repository, where some critical Playwright flows intermittently fail due to timing and environment orchestration issues. Those issues appear mitigated but not fully eradicated.

Final Sign-Off Statement
Quality sign-off is conditionally recommended: backend services, route protection, booking validation, tour search/filtering, and cascade integrity show acceptable quality based on automated evidence; final production sign-off should include one stable environment rerun of the critical Playwright workflow pack to confirm frontend orchestration consistency.
